Motivation: 现有模型压缩技术（如SparseGPT等权值稀疏化方法）虽然能高效部署LLM，但会放大模型中的已有偏见，导致输出随提示中的人口特征线索显著变化，因此需要在剪枝过程中考虑去偏。

Method: 提出Debias-SparseGPT后训练剪枝方法，在SparseGPT基础上引入基于人口统计对比输入（demographically contrasting inputs）定义的二阶项进行表征去偏；在最具限制性的稀疏模式下，进一步使用长上下文、内容丰富（long-context, content-rich）的样本扩充校准集。

Result: 在多种生成式LLM和多种稀疏度（25%、50%、结构化2:4）下，Debias-SparseGPT相比SparseGPT持续降低剪枝诱导偏差，同时保持模型困惑度和零样本准确率；在2:4结构化稀疏下，扩充校准集可同时改善下游性能和公平性。

Conclusion: Debias-SparseGPT在保持稀疏模型计算效率的同时，推进了偏差-性能的权衡优化（bias-performance trade-off），实现了既高效又更公平的模型压缩。

Abstract: Model compression techniques such as pruning and quantization facilitate the efficient deployment and acceleration of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies show that weight sparsification methods, such as SparseGPT, can amplify existing biases in models, with outputs varying significantly depending on persona cues in the prompt. In this paper, we introduce Debias-SparseGPT, a post-training pruning method incorporating representational debiasing using a second-order term defined over demographically contrasting inputs. We perform empirical validation of our method over a wide range of generative LLMs. Across models and sparsity regimes (25%, 50%, and structured 2:4 sparsity), Debias-SparseGPT consistently reduces pruning-induced bias compared to SparseGPT while preserving model perplexity and zero-shot accuracy. Under the most restrictive 2:4 structured sparsity pattern, which most aggressively degrades model quality, augmenting the calibration set with long-context, content-rich examples further improves both downstream performance and fairness. Overall, Debias-SparseGPT advances the bias-performance trade-off while preserving the computational efficiency of sparse models.

</details>


### [163] [When Persona Attributes Improve Population Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02526)
*Leon Fröhling,Jens Rupprecht,Markus Strohmaier,Claudia Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）中"角色提示"（persona prompting）技术在调查预测任务中的有效性，提出人类对调查问题的响应变异性是导致过往结果好坏参半的潜在解释，并系统比较了不同角色属性选择方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色提示技术在LLM调查预测中已被广泛应用，但现有研究结果混杂且不一致，缺乏清晰的成功与失败模式。尤其是不同属性选择方法的表现差异及其选择依据仍不明确，需要系统性研究来厘清。

Method: 提出将人类对调查问题的响应变异性作为混合表现的潜在解释，并在两个国家、四种综合社会调查、六个LLM和每个调查二十个预测任务上，系统比较不同角色属性选择方法的角色提示性能。

Result: 研究帮助识别了角色提示在调查预测任务中何时可能有用，并为LLM基于角色提示的调查预测中不同属性选择方法的有效性提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 人类响应的变异性可能是解释角色提示混合表现的重要因素；不同属性选择方法的有效性存在差异，研究为实践中如何选择属性提供了指导性见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to predict the responses of human participants in survey panels. Towards that goal, persona prompting has recently emerged as a technique to inform and align large pretrained language models. Persona prompting refers to the practice of using short textual descriptions of 'personas' in prompts to steer the LLM's generations. Personas describe individuals through different attributes such as their socio-demographics, attitudes, or behaviors, with the aim of aligning LLMs to produce responses that correlate with the corresponding human responses. Yet, recent work has produced mixed and partly conflicting results of persona prompting without clear patterns of success and failure. Among the few consistent findings is that the selection of persona attributes matters, and that using more attributes does not necessarily lead to better performance. It remains unclear how different attribute selection methods perform and how to choose among them. In this paper, we propose that observed human response variation of a survey question is a potential explanation for the mixed performance observed so far. In addition, we compare the performance of persona prompting associated with different methods for selecting persona attributes. We evaluate these methods on four different (general) social surveys across two countries, six LLMs, and twenty prediction tasks per survey. Our work helps to identify when persona prompting can be expected to be useful in survey prediction tasks, and provides new insights on the effectiveness of different attribute selection methods for LLM-based survey prediction using persona prompting.

</details>


### [164] [Predictors of Loneliness in Older Adults Using Multimodal Analysis of Speech and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02606)
*Vinmay Khandode,Sai Karthik Kosuri,Neil K. R. Sehgal,Adam Greene,Elif Alpoge,Elana Duffy,Matthew Lee Smith,Thomas K. M. Cudjoe,Sharath Chandra Guntuku*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分析310名老年人的半结构化电话访谈，利用多模态框架（结合语言特征与声学特征）检测老年人孤独感，发现多模态模型（r=0.298）优于单一文本或音频模型，表明孤独感通过语言内容和声音特征共同表现，可用于心理评估的辅助早期指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤独感是老年人面临的重大公共健康问题，与抑郁、认知衰退和死亡风险相关，但当前缺乏可扩展、客观的检测方法，特别是在自然对话情境中。

Method: 对310名老年人进行半结构化电话访谈，构建多模态分析框架：结合心理语言学词典、n-gram和主题模型等语言特征，以及音高、语调、音量等声学特征，分析其与自我报告的孤独感评分之间的关联；采用预定义和数据驱动两种方法，并对比多模态模型与文本/音频单一模型的性能。

Result: 较高孤独感与否定词（r=0.11）、消极语调（r=0.12）及冲突相关语言相关；较低孤独感与社会参照（r=-0.18）、动机驱动（r=-0.11）和言语情感丰富度（r=-0.12）相关。多模态模型（r=0.298）优于纯文本和纯音频模型。

Conclusion: 孤独感通过语言内容和声学线索共同体现，支持基于语音的分析在心理评估中的潜力；建议将其作为现有评估工具的辅助早期指标，而非独立的诊断工具。

Abstract: Loneliness is a critical public health issue among older adults, linked to higher risks of depression, cognitive decline, and mortality. Scalable, objective methods for its detection remain limited, particularly in natural conversational contexts. We analyzed speech and language markers of loneliness in 310 older adults using semi-structured telephone interviews to help understand how they process feeling lonely and how their language differs at different levels of feeling loneliness. Our multimodal framework combined linguistic features (psycholinguistic dictionaries, n-grams, and topic models) with acoustic features (pitch, tone, loudness) to examine associations with self-reported loneliness scores. Both predefined and data-driven methods captured patterns in verbal content and vocal delivery. Higher loneliness was associated with negations(r = 0.11), negative tone(r = 0.12), and conflict-related language. Lower loneliness was linked to social references(r = -0.18), motivational drives(r = -0.11), and emotional richness in speech(r = -0.12). We also found that the multimodal model (r = 0.298) outperforms the text-only and audio-only models. Findings suggest that loneliness manifests through both linguistic and acoustic cues, supporting the potential of speech-based analysis in psychological assessments and as an early indicator of emotional loneliness when used alongside existing assessments, rather than as standalone diagnostic tools.

</details>


### [165] [TaRA: Training-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02639)
*Taehyeon Kim,Eunhyeok Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TaRA（训练感知的低秩适配初始化）方法，通过使低秩因子产生的梯度逼近全秩权重矩阵的梯度来初始化LoRA，在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下显著提升微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA是参数高效微调的标准方法，但其性能受初始化影响很大，因为低秩分解引入了信息瓶颈。现有初始化方法利用预训练权重、激活或梯度的主成分，但未直接考虑全秩模型的训练动态，导致初始化质量受限。

Method: TaRA通过数学推导，初始化LoRA时使低秩因子诱导的梯度能够紧密逼近对应全秩权重矩阵的梯度，从训练动态的角度提升梯度保真度，且引入的计算开销可忽略不计。

Result: 在多个多样且具有挑战性的微调任务上，TaRA一致性地优于先前的先进方法（SOTA），表现出简单、鲁棒和可扩展的特性。

Conclusion: TaRA提供了一种简单、鲁棒且可扩展的LoRA初始化方案，通过直接建模训练过程中的梯度动态，有效解决了LoRA初始化的关键瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a de facto standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), yet its performance is highly sensitive to initialization due to the information bottleneck imposed by low-rank decomposition. Existing approaches attempt to construct high-quality LoRA initializations by exploiting principal components of pretrained weights, activations, or gradients. However, these methods do not directly account for the training dynamics of the full-rank model. In this paper, we propose Training-aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization (TaRA), a method that initializes LoRA such that the gradients induced by the low-rank factors closely approximate the gradient of the corresponding full-rank weight matrix. Derived from a mathematical formulation, TaRA improves gradient fidelity at the start of training while introducing negligible computational overhead. Across diverse and challenging fine-tuning tasks, TaRA consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods, establishing a simple, robust, and scalable solution for effective LoRA initialization.

</details>


### [166] [WinoQueer-NL: Assessing Bias in Dutch Language Models toward LGBTQ+ Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02651)
*Jiska Beuk,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了首个基于荷兰语文化和语言环境的反同性恋偏见基准数据集，通过43名荷兰酷儿参与者的在线调查验证并扩展了英语WinoQueer基准，最终发布包含42,906个句子的数据集。评估发现荷兰语模型的平均偏见分数看似中性（~50%），但细分显示跨性别和非二元身份群体持续获得最高偏见分数，强调了文化校准数据集对评估和缓解语言模型偏见的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英语语言模型中的反酷儿偏见已被广泛研究，但荷兰语模型在此领域仍是空白。现有英语基准无法直接套用于荷兰语场景，缺乏文化和语言适配的评估工具，导致荷兰语语言模型可能存在的偏见未被发现和缓解。

Method: 基于英语WinoQueer基准进行文化和语言适配，构建荷兰语数据集；通过在线调查招募43名荷兰酷儿参与者验证171个刻板印象中的145个并收集22个新偏见；最终数据集含42,906个句子，使用多种荷兰语专用及多语言模型（包括MLM和ARLM）进行评测，通过比较刻板与非刻板句子的对数似然计算偏见分数。

Result: 模型平均偏见分数接近中性（~50%），但细分分析显示显著差异：某些模型对跨性别身份有高达97%的时间偏好刻板句子，对同性恋相关配对仅6%的时间如此；跨性别和非二元身份群体在所有模型中持续获得最高偏见分数。

Conclusion: 文化校准的数据集对评估和缓解语言模型偏见至关重要，荷兰语模型对跨性别和非二元身份的偏见尤为严重，需要进一步关注和缓解。

Abstract: While English language models have been widely examined for anti-queer bias, Dutch models remain understudied. To address this gap, we developed a culturally and linguistically adapted Dutch dataset based on the English WinoQueer benchmark, containing pairs of stereotypical and counter-stereotypical sentences. To validate and expand it, we conducted an online survey with 43 Dutch queer participants, confirming 145 of 171 stereotypes as culturally relevant and identifying 22 new biases through free-text responses. The final released dataset, comprising 42,906 sentences, was evaluated using a range of Dutch-specific and multilingual models, including both masked language models (MLMs) and autoregressive language models (ARLMs), with bias measured via a score comparing log-likelihoods of stereotypical versus counter-stereotypical sentences. While the mean bias score across models appeared neutral (~50%), closer analysis revealed significant disparities: some models favored stereotypical sentences up to 97% of the time for transgender identities, but only 6% of the time for gay-related pairs, with transgender and non-binary identities consistently receiving the highest bias scores. Our findings highlight the importance of culturally grounded datasets for evaluating and mitigating biases that disproportionately impact marginalized groups in Dutch language models.

</details>


### [167] [oHC: Orthogonal Hyper-Connections on SO(4) via Quaternions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02672)
*Haoqiang Guo,Xuyi Chen,Bo Ke,Yishu Lei,Ziyang Xu,Shikun Feng,Ximen,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出正交超连接（oHC），将残差矩阵限制在旋转群SO(n)上，解决了流形约束超连接（mHC）中残差流衰减和多样性丢失的问题，在多项下游任务中优于基线、mHC和iHC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyper-Connections将Transformer的单残差流替换为n个并行流，每层通过学习到的n×n残差矩阵混合。无约束矩阵会放大残差信号导致训练不稳定；mHC将矩阵限制在双随机矩阵上虽然限制了上限，但允许下限趋近于零，导致残差流之间的差异随层深收缩、多样性被耗尽。

Method: 提出将残差矩阵限制在旋转群SO(n)上，使混合步骤在任何方向上既不放大也不衰减残差流。在n=4时，利用一对单位四元数以闭合形式参数化该群，不增加参数，用固定的符号加减模式替代迭代投影，构造速度比mHC更快。

Result: 在全面的下游任务集上评估，oHC优于单残差流基线、mHC以及将残差矩阵固定为单位矩阵的iHC。

Conclusion: 正交约束是超连接中更优的矩阵限制策略，既保证训练稳定又不损失残差流的多样性表达能力。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) replace the single residual stream of a Transformer with $n$ parallel ones, mixing them at every layer with a learned $n \times n$ residual matrix. Leaving that matrix unconstrained places no limit on the factor by which the mixing step rescales the residual streams, and that factor compounds across layers, which destabilizes training. Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) address this by restricting the matrix to the doubly stochastic matrices. That caps the factor at one, so the mixing can no longer amplify any direction, but nothing bounds it from below. We prove that inside this set the mixing step can reduce the norm of the residual streams only by shrinking the differences between the streams, while their mean is left unchanged; and since the reduction accumulates over layers, the streams grow more alike and their diversity is spent with depth. We therefore propose Orthogonal Hyper-Connections (oHC), restricting the residual matrix to the rotation group $SO(n)$, so that the mixing step can neither amplify nor attenuate the residual streams in any direction, which keeps training stable and no longer forces the differences between the streams to contract. Specifically, at the four streams used by recent HC models we parameterize the group in closed form by a pair of unit quaternions, which adds no parameters, replaces the iterative projection with a fixed pattern of signed additions, and can be constructed faster than mHC. We evaluate oHC across a comprehensive set of downstream tasks, where it outperforms the single-stream residual baseline, mHC and iHC, which fixes the residual matrix to the identity.

</details>


### [168] [From Tokens to Semantics: Leveraging Complementary Signals for Hallucination Detection in Black-Box LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02679)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Owen O'Neill,Nabeel Kemal,Abhishek Mandal,Houssem Chatbri,Christopher Martin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在缺乏可信上下文时如何利用黑盒API信号检测幻觉/幻觉，比较了语义熵和token不确定性两类信号及其混合方法，提出了TopK、Gated和Stacked等方法，在7个基准（含多模态）和4个模型上评估，发现Stacked性能最佳但无通用最强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当LLM支撑面向公众或高风险工作流时，漏检的捏造内容会伤害用户和机构，而误报则会浪费有限的人工审核资源。在缺乏可信上下文或参考文档的场景下，需要有效的幻觉检测信号。

Method: 扩展了基于token的不确定性检测（TopK方法，聚合采样响应中的token级信号）；评估了结合目标响应不确定性与语义不相似性的混合CoCoA方法；提出了两种监督方法：Gated（将单簇情况路由至聚合token特征分类器）和Stacked（联合学习语义不确定性和更广泛的token特征）。

Result: 在7个基准（5个公开基准含文本和手写支票多模态提取，2个构造基准）和4个语言模型上评估发现：Stacked在近一半案例中表现最佳；TopK和CoCoA无需监督标签即有竞争力但阈值需要仔细校准；无任何方法在所有情况下都最强。

Conclusion: 在1%-15%的假阳性率预算下评估各方法性能，分析其对生成和校准选择的敏感性，并考察数据集特征导致的性能差异，揭示了不同方法失败模式的互补性及其适用边界。

Abstract: When LLMs support public-facing or high-stakes workflows, missed fabrications can harm users and institutions, while false alarms consume limited human-review capacity. When no trusted context or reference document is available, we study two signals accessible through black-box model APIs: semantic entropy, which measures disagreement among sampled response meanings, and uncertainty derived from token log-probabilities. Their failure modes can be complementary: semantic entropy becomes uninformative when responses form one semantic cluster, while token uncertainty can miss consistently confident errors. We extend token-based uncertainty detection by aggregating token-level signals across sampled responses through our TopK method, evaluate the hybrid CoCoA method, which combines target-response uncertainty with semantic dissimilarity, and propose and study two supervised methods: Gated, which routes single-cluster cases to an aggregated-token-feature classifier, and Stacked, which learns jointly from semantic uncertainty and broader token features. We evaluate seven benchmarks, including five public benchmarks (four text datasets and multimodal handwritten-cheque extraction) and two constructed benchmarks (Financial Summaries and Long-Text QA), using four language models. In our evaluation across models and datasets, Stacked gave the best performance in nearly half of the cases, while TopK and CoCoA remain competitive without supervised training labels, although their thresholds require careful calibration. No method is universally strongest. We therefore evaluate performance at false-positive-rate budgets from 1% to 15%, assess their sensitivity to generation and calibration choices, and examine variation across dataset characteristics.

</details>


### [169] [DKL: Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02685)
*Kushagra Bhushan,Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Gaurav Pandey,Sonam Gupta,Vineet Kumar,Jaydeep Sen,Yatin Nandwani,Sachindra Joshi,Dinesh Raghu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DKL（解耦知识学习）方法，通过在对应的基础LLM上执行扩展预训练注入新知识，再通过模型合并将知识权重与指令微调LLM融合，避免昂贵的指令微调，同时保持模型的指令跟随能力。在检索失败场景下，DKL将RAG准确率从54.17提升至79.26，训练数据量大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法在检索错误或不完整时会产生幻觉；RAFT和PA-RAG等微调方法需要生成覆盖整个语料库的海量合成QA数据，成本高昂；扩展预训练虽避免了合成数据需求但会损害指令跟随能力，且后续指令微调成本高甚至因缺乏指令微调语料而不可行。

Method: DKL的核心思路是将知识注入与指令跟随能力解耦。具体做法是：不在指令微调LLM上直接进行扩展预训练，而是在其对应的基础LLM上进行扩展预训练以注入新知识，然后将注入知识的权重与指令微调LLM进行模型合并，从而在不破坏指令跟随能力的前提下传递新知识。该方法轻量且无需昂贵的指令微调。

Result: 在检索失败场景下，DKL将RAG准确率从54.17提升到79.26，且相较于先前方法在显著更少的训练数据下取得了更优的效果。

Conclusion: DKL通过解耦知识学习和指令跟随能力的训练，利用模型合并技术实现了对新知识的有效注入，同时保持了指令跟随性能，是一种更高效、更经济的替代方案。

Abstract: RAG has become the de facto method for incorporating new, corpus-specific knowledge into an instruction following LLM (Instruct LLM). Although RAG-based prompting improves factual grounding, it fails when retrieval is incorrect or incomplete, leading to hallucinations. Finetuning methods such as RAFT and PA-RAG enhance RAG by injecting new knowledge into the model's parameters, but require generating a massive amount of synthetic QA that covers the entire corpus. Extended Pre-Training (EPT) on the text corpus avoids the need for comprehensive synthetic data generation but compromises an Instruct LLM's instruction-following capabilities, necessitating instruction fine-tuning (IFT) after pre-training. However, IFT is costly and may be infeasible due to the unavailability of an instruction-tuning corpus. In this work, we propose DKL-Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models. Instead of doing EPT on the Instruct LLM, DKL performs EPT on its corresponding base LLM to infuse new knowledge. These knowledge infused weights are then merged with the Instruct LLM, imparting new knowledge without affecting their instruction-following capabilities. DKL is a lightweight method that avoids expensive instruction fine-tuning and relies on model merging to infuse the new knowledge into the Instruct LLM without destroying its instruction following capabilities. Empirical results show that DKL improves RAG accuracy from 54.17 to 79.26 on retrieval failure cases, while outperforming prior approaches with substantially less training data.

</details>


### [170] [Trace as State: Reasoning Traces as Conditional States for Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02702)
*Xu Zou,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"Trace as State"方法，利用推理轨迹作为任务状态的文本代理，将其放置在长上下文之前以引导重读，解决了因果Transformer在处理需要后期才知道状态的长上下文推理时的"事后发现"不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的因果处理机制与长上下文推理存在根本性矛盾：许多推理任务依赖的条件状态只有在上下文末尾才出现，而因果架构需要顺序处理信息，这种"任务状态事后才知道"的问题可能导致严重的记忆开销（最坏情况下指数级增长）。

Method: 首先形式化定义了"条件状态更新任务"，证明因果状态更新处理器在条件最后出现时需要指数级更多内存。基于此原理，提出"Trace as State"方法：将已收集的推理轨迹作为任务状态的文本代理，放置在长上下文块之前进行新一轮读取，让因果Transformer能先利用先前推导出的信息来指导后续内容的重新阅读。对照实验条件为"Trace Append"，即将同样的状态代理放在上下文之后。

Result: 在3个模型和3个长上下文数据集上，共27种模型-任务-指标组合中，Trace as State在26种组合中优于Trace Append。在GraphWalks Parents任务上，DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview的精确匹配率从初始29.2%"和Trace Append的43.0%"提升至81.8%；GLM-5.2从66.4%"和83.2%"提升至100.0%。

Conclusion: 将推理轨迹放在上下文之前能够显著提升长上下文推理能力，且无需改变因果Transformer的基本结构，为长上下文推理提供了一种简单有效且理论上可解释的改进策略。

Abstract: Transformers process information causally, but long-context reasoning may depend on task state discovered only later. We formalize this mismatch through conditional state update tasks. For causal state update processors, providing the condition first can require exponentially less memory in the worst case than providing it last.
  Motivated by this principle, we introduce Trace as State. We use collected reasoning traces as a textual proxy for task state and place it before the long-context block on a fresh pass, allowing information derived previously to guide rereading.
  We conduct extensive experiments on Trace as State and Trace Append, a matched control that uses the same task state proxy but put it after the context. Across three models and three long-context datasets, Trace as State outperforms Trace Append in 26 of 27 reported combinations of model, task, and metric. On GraphWalks Parents, exact match lifts DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview from 29.2% on the initial pass and 43.0% with Trace Appendto 81.8% with Trace as State, and from 66.4% and 83.2% to 100.0% for GLM-5.2. These results show that placing traces before the context can improve long-context reasoning while retaining the causal transformer structure.

</details>


### [171] [CORAL: An LLM-Native Harness for Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02730)
*Muhammad Rafay Azhar,Yuhang Zhou,Gilbert Jiang,Yuchen Wang,Rahul Sharma,Matthew DeSousa,Jiayi Liu,Xin Guo,Lizhu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CORAL，一个基于LLM的智能体闭环框架，用于自动持续优化推荐系统。该智能体在每个周期中观察运行信号、基于历史决策与结果的记忆进行推理，并通过工具（包括数值优化器）在固定运行预算内重新配置推荐器，测量到的结果反馈到下一周期，无需参数更新即可在上下文中改进策略。在两个大型社交平台上的A/B实验中，CORAL分别在不增加服务成本的情况下提升参与度，以及在保持参与度的情况下降低服务成本，横跨参与度-效率前沿，且随循环迭代性能持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统优化依赖人工工程师通过在线实验进行测试，这是一个缓慢、被动且受工程资源限制的过程，导致系统在内容、用户行为和上游模型不断变化时难以被及时修订。虽然大语言模型已被应用于排序、用户建模和离线模型开发，但很少有系统将智能体置于持续闭环中，使其能够对实时推荐系统采取行动并从决策的测量效果中学习。

Method: CORAL将推荐系统持续优化建模为部分可观测、非平稳、带约束的优化问题。每轮循环中，LLM智能体：(1)观察系统运行信号；(2)基于历史决策与结果的记忆进行推理；(3)调用工具（包括数值优化器）在固定运行预算内重新配置推荐系统；(4)将实测结果反馈至下一周期。策略无需参数更新，仅通过先前的行动在上下文中不断改进。

Result: 在两个大型社交平台上进行的A/B实验表明，同一套CORAL框架在一个平台上不增加服务成本即可提升参与度，在另一个平台上不降低参与度即可减少服务成本，成功跨越了参与度-效率前沿线。随循环迭代，系统性能持续改善。

Conclusion: CORAL证明了一个单一的智能体闭环能够自动化传统上由人类算法工程师在明确防护措施下执行的持续优化工作，为推荐系统的在线持续优化提供了全新范式，在明确的运行预算和安全护栏下实现全自动化的系统调优。

Abstract: Production recommender systems shape what billions of people see, and sustaining their performance requires continual optimization: as content, user behavior, and upstream models shift, the choices governing retrieval, ranking, and serving must be revisited. Traditionally, human engineers test such changes through online experiments--a slow, reactive process limited by engineering effort, leaving parts of the system unrevised as conditions change. Although large language models have been applied to ranking, user modeling, and offline model development, few systems place an agent in a continual closed loop that acts on a live recommender and learns from the measured effects of its decisions. We present CORAL (Constraint-Optimized Recommender via an Agentic Loop), an LLM-native harness that closes this loop: each cycle, the agent observes operating signals, reasons over a memory of past decisions and outcomes, and invokes tools--including a numerical optimizer that keeps changes within a fixed operating budget--to reconfigure the recommender, with measured outcomes informing the next cycle. We formulate this as a partially observed, non-stationary, constrained optimization problem in which the policy improves in context, without parameter updates, from its prior actions. Across two large-scale social platforms, evaluated with A/B experiments, the same harness improves engagement at no additional serving cost on one and reduces serving cost without degrading engagement on the other, spanning the engagement-efficiency frontier. Performance improves as the loop iterates, suggesting that a single agentic loop can automate continual optimization work traditionally performed by human algorithm engineers under explicit guardrails.

</details>


### [172] [Choosing a PEFT Variant for Per-Patient Dysarthric ASR: A Single-Speaker Case Study on Two ASR Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02735)
*Bernard Muller,László Tóth,LaVonne Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对构音障碍ASR的每患者适配场景，在单一后卒中匈牙利语男性重度构音障碍患者（S1）上系统比较了七种LoRA家族参数高效微调方法（LoRA、QLoRA、AdaLoRA、DoRA、LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA），并评估了两种生产级基础模型（Whisper-large-v3和Qwen3-ASR-1.7B）。结果显示LoRA与DoRA无显著差异（p>0.5），因此选择更简单廉价的LoRA；真实4-bit QLoRA在所有种子和基础模型上均更差且无内存节省；LoHA、VeRA等未达LoRA家族水平。全参数微调虽更准确（11.43% CER），但扩展至前馈块的115 MB LoRA仅差0.66个百分点，存储仅为全量微调的约3.7%。语音样本量研究表明约5分钟患者音频即可获得零样本到30分钟CER降幅的45.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每患者适配器是构音障碍语音识别生产架构的首选，但在说话人依赖的每患者场景下，参数高效微调（PEFT）变体尚未被系统比较过。已有研究通常针对说话人通用或多说话人场景，缺乏单说话人逐患者条件下的PEFT方法系统对比，导致生产实践中方法选择缺乏实证依据。

Method: 采用单说话人案例研究方法，对一名后卒中匈牙利语男性重度构音障碍患者（S1，409条话语）进行实验。在两种生产基础模型（带匈牙利语微调的Whisper-large-v3和多语言Qwen3-ASR-1.7B）上，比较七种LoRA家族方法。使用三重种子、配对bootstrap统计检验评估LoRA与DoRA差异。同时测试QLoRA的真实4-bit量化、扩展至前馈块的LoRA变体，以及通过6点语音量网格评估患者音频时长对性能的影响。

Result: 注意力投影适配器在两种基础模型上均显著改善CER。LoRA与DoRA无显著差异（Whisper：13.86/13.90%，Qwen3-ASR：28.10/28.33%）。真实4-bit QLoRA在所有条件下更差（14.56/30.09%）且无内存节省。LoHA在Whisper上仍带来18.6%相对CER降低。全微调达11.43% CER，但适配前馈层的LoRA（115 MB）达到12.09%，仅差0.66个百分点，存储仅约3.7%。约5分钟患者音频捕获45.6%的零样本至30分钟CER降幅，10和30分钟仍有进一步提升。

Conclusion: 在单说话人每患者构音障碍ASR场景中，LoRA是LoRA家族中最优的实用选择（与DoRA性能相当但更简单廉价）；应避免使用真实4-bit QLoRA；可通过适配前馈层缩小与全微调的差距同时大幅节省存储；约5分钟患者语音即可获得大部分适配收益。研究限制为单说话人、单语言、重度后卒中构音障碍，结论需谨慎推广。

Abstract: Per-patient adapters are the preferred production architecture for dysarthric automatic speech recognition (ASR), yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) variants have not been compared in the speaker-dependent, per-patient regime. We present a single-speaker case study comparing seven LoRA-family methods (LoRA, QLoRA, AdaLoRA, DoRA, LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA) on two production bases (Whisper-large-v3 with Hungarian fine-tuning, and a multilingual Qwen3-ASR-1.7B checkpoint) for one post-stroke Hungarian male speaker (S1, 409 utterances; severe dysarthria on auditory-perceptual clinical assessment). Attention-projection adapters substantially improve CER on both bases. Across three seeds, a paired bootstrap detects no significant LoRA-DoRA difference (p>0.5; 13.86/13.90 % CER on Whisper, 28.10/28.33 % on Qwen3-ASR), so we adopt the simpler, cheaper LoRA. Real 4-bit (NF4) QLoRA is worse on every seed and both bases (14.56/30.09 % CER) with no memory saving at this scale, and LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA and AdaLoRA do not reach the LoRA family, though LoHA still gives an 18.6 % relative CER reduction on Whisper. On the same base, full fine-tuning is more accurate (11.43 % CER), but a 115 MB LoRA that also adapts the feed-forward blocks reaches within 0.66 pp of it at approximately 3.7 % of the per-patient storage. A 6-point enrollment grid shows about 5 min of patient audio captures 45.6 % of the zero-shot-to-30-min CER reduction, with further gains at 10 and 30 min (caveat: one speaker, one language, severe post-stroke dysarthria). Training scripts and recipes will be released, source-available under a research-use licence, on publication.

</details>


### [173] [Language Models Can Control Their Own Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02737)
*Namgyu Ho,Huzama Ahmad,Woosung Koh,Se-Young Yun,Tal Schuster,Cicero Nogueira dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出声明式注意力（Declarative Attention, DA），一种让模型在思维链中主动声明需要关注的上下文区域的协议，将生成划分为全局、聚焦和局部三种模式，从而跳过大部分KV缓存读取，在15个长上下文任务上实现52%和31%的注意力token减少，准确率仅下降1.27和2.75个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在生成回复时需读取整个KV缓存来查找少数关键token，传统方法通过外部代理评分预选相关token，但仍需O(N)的扫描开销。作者提出一个根本性问题：模型本身是否已经知道上下文的哪些部分相关？从而探索从外部评分转向模型内在声明的范式转变。

Method: 提出声明式注意力（DA）协议：让模型在思维链中像"工具调用"一样解析式地声明注意力需求，将生成过程划分为三种模式——全局模式（完整上下文）、聚焦模式（特定区域）和局部模式（仅最近输出）。推理引擎通过解析这些声明来跳过大部分KV缓存读取。

Result: 在15个长上下文任务的零样本评估中，DA在现成模型（Gemma-4-31B和Qwen-3.6-27B）上使解码期间总注意力token减少52.0%和31.1%，准确率仅下降1.27pp和2.75pp，且性能下降随模型规模增大而减小。

Conclusion: DA开辟了稀疏注意力的新维度，验证了模型自身具备声明注意力需求能力的假设，并为未来的基于训练的方法提供了探索空间，兼顾推理效率与精度保持。

Abstract: Language models spend most of their attention on a small fraction of context, yet they read the entire KV cache to find the few tokens that matter. If the user asks about a previous detail in a 1M-token conversation, global attention layers must scan the full context to generate each token of the reply. A prominent approach mitigates this cost by pre-selecting relevant tokens via lightweight proxy scores, but this extrinsic scoring still incurs O(N) per step. We take an intrinsic approach motivated by the simple question: wouldn't the model already know which parts of the context are relevant? To this end, we introduce Declarative Attention (DA), a protocol that elicits the model to declare where it needs to attend within its chain-of-thought, partitioning generation into three modes: <global> (full context), <focus> (a specific region), and <local> (recent output only). The inference engine parses these declarations like tool calls and skips most of the KV cache read. Under zero-shot evaluation across 15 long-context tasks, DA on off-the-shelf models (Gemma-4-31B, Qwen-3.6-27B) significantly reduces total attended tokens during decoding (52.0%, 31.1%) with modest accuracy drops (1.27pp, 2.75pp) that shrink with model scale. DA unlocks a new axis of sparse attention, with further potential under training-based methods that future work can explore.

</details>


### [174] [Untangling the Mechanisms of Misleading Context in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02754)
*Robin Linzmayer,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了误导性上下文（虚假证据与断言）如何破坏大语言模型的医学判断。基于MedMisBench基准（8,627个问题），三个推理模型对断言的采纳频率比对虚假证据高10-27个百分点，但断言在推理轨迹中被披露的频率更低（7-90%应答vs 81-98%轨迹）。两种误导机制不同：证据在早期逐步累积影响，断言则在结尾重定向结论。LLM监控器在阅读开放模型的推理轨迹时能捕获78%的受损决策（假阳性率5%），而仅看应答最多只能捕获32%。结论：模型最易受误导的上下文披露最少，且只有开放推理轨迹才能可靠监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能以专家水平回答医学问题，但其行动的上下文可能具有误导性，而误导性上下文会破坏模型的医学判断。理解这种破坏机制对于AI安全至关重要。

Method: 使用MedMisBench基准（含8,627个经临床医生审查的问题），注入两种误导性上下文线索（虚构证据和直接断言），测试三个推理模型（两个暴露完整推理轨迹，一个前沿模型仅暴露应答），并从易感性、披露性、推理机制和可监控性四个维度进行分析。

Result: 所有三个模型对断言的易感性高于对虚假证据（高出10-27个百分点）。误导线索在推理轨迹中的披露率（81-98%）远高于应答（7-90%），且断言的披露率低于证据类线索。两种线索的机制不同：证据早期进入逐步累积，断言在推理结尾重定向结论。LLM监控器在读取开放模型推理轨迹时可捕获78%的受损决策（5%假阳性率），而任何基于应答的监控最多只能捕获32%。

Conclusion: 模型最易受误导的上下文类型（断言）披露程度最低，且只有在开放推理轨迹中才能被可靠捕获，而前沿模型提供商不公开推理轨迹。这揭示了可监控性与模型能力权衡中的重大安全缺口。

Abstract: Large language models now answer medical questions with expert-level performance. However, the context these systems act on can be misleading, and misleading context can corrupt a model's medical judgment. To understand how misleading context corrupts this judgment, we examine the model's susceptibility to the context, disclosure of it, mechanism of corrupted reasoning, and monitorability of the decision. On the medical reasoning subset of MedMisBench, a clinician-reviewed question-answering benchmark of 8,627 questions, we inject two types of misleading context cues, fabricated evidence and a bare assertion. We test three reasoning models, two that expose their full reasoning trace and one frontier model that exposes only its response. All three are more susceptible to the assertion than to the fabricated evidence, adopting the asserted answer 10 to 27 points more often. The misleading cues are disclosed in 81 to 98% of traces but only 7 to 90% of responses, and the assertion is disclosed less often than evidence based cues. Resampling from reasoning traces without disclosure shows the two cues corrupt reasoning differently, evidence entering early and accumulating while the assertion redirects the conclusion near its end. An LLM monitor catches 78% of corrupted decisions at 5% false positives when reading an open model's trace with guidance, against at most 32% from any response. The misleading context that models are most susceptible to is disclosed least, and was caught reliably only from an open reasoning trace, which frontier providers withhold.

</details>


### [175] [From Reweighting to Rewriting: Unlocking the Intervention Effects of Influential Samples in Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02771)
*Yuzhang Luo,Chenpeng Wang,Jianhui Chen,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出影响力引导的响应重写（影响函数选择干预目标并替换其监督输出），与传统的重加权干预相比，在相同的影响函数选中样本上，响应重写能产生更强、更持久和双向的行为转变，而重加权效果微弱且不稳定，揭示了影响函数估计的局部重加权效应与其识别样本的整体干预潜力之间存在区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据归因（TDA）的干预价值既取决于选择的样本，也取决于修改方式。传统影响函数选择的样本在基于重加权的干预下相比随机选择优势有限，这引发疑问：是有影响样本本身缺乏干预价值，还是重加权方式未能释放其行为杠杆？文章旨在区分这两种可能性。

Method: 引入影响引导的响应重写方法：使用影响函数识别干预目标样本，在保持指令不变的前提下，将其响应替换为行为对齐或行为对立的监督信号。在四个开源大语言模型上，以认知弃权（epistemic abstention）为主要测试场景，对同一批影响函数选中的样本，系统比较响应重写与传统重加权两种干预方式的效果。

Result: 响应重写产生更强、更持久且双向的行为转变，而相同样本上的重加权效果微弱且不一致；影响函数选中的样本比替代选择器提供更大的重写杠杆，且行为变化集中在目标相关行为上。该定性对比在安全拒绝（safety refusal）任务上同样成立。

Conclusion: 影响估计捕捉的局部重加权效应与其识别出的样本所具备的更广泛干预杠杆存在本质区别，这推动了面向干预感知的TDA方法评估。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) aims to identify training examples that shape model behavior, but its intervention value depends on both which examples are selected and how they are modified. Influence functions (IF) estimate behavioral changes under infinitesimal reweighting, yet IF-selected examples often show limited advantages over random selection under conventional weight-based interventions. This raises the question of whether influential examples lack intervention value or whether reweighting fails to realize their behavioral leverage.We introduce influence-guided response rewriting, which uses IF to identify intervention targets and replaces their responses with behavior-aligned or behavior-opposed supervision while keeping instructions fixed. Across four open-weight LLMs, we compare rewriting and reweighting on the same influence-selected examples using epistemic abstention as our primary testbed. Response rewriting produces stronger, more persistent, and bidirectional behavioral shifts, while reweighting the same examples yields weak and inconsistent effects. Further analyses show that influence-selected examples provide greater rewriting leverage than alternative selectors, with changes remaining concentrated on target-relevant behaviors. The same qualitative contrast extends to safety refusal. These results distinguish the local reweighting effects captured by influence estimates from the broader intervention leverage of the examples they identify, motivating intervention-aware evaluation of TDA methods.

</details>


### [176] [HyperStyler: Low-resource Authorship Style Transfer via Context-aware Style Navigation and Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02772)
*Jongkyung Shin,Minguk Jeon,Chanwoo Park,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyperStyler将低资源作者风格迁移解耦为风格选择与风格实现两个子任务，通过预测风格坐标并用动态参数调制替代隐状态注入，在仅增加2.4%参数的情况下优于包括大语言模型在内的现有方法，推理速度比大语言模型快1.8倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低资源作者风格迁移方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是将多样化的参考样例压缩为单一静态的作者嵌入，导致上下文相关的风格变化被平均化；二是依赖隐状态表示进行风格控制，使风格与内容相互纠缠。这两点使得方法难以同时保证较高的风格保真度和语义保持。

Method: 提出HyperStyler架构，将LAST任务解耦为两个子任务：(1) Stylo-navigator负责联合建模源上下文和目标作者参考样例，预测风格坐标；(2) Stylo-hypernet通过动态参数调制（而非隐状态注入）来实现风格坐标所对应的风格效果。

Result: 在Reddit、Blog和News三个数据集上的实验表明，HyperStyler持续优于包括基于大语言模型方法在内的现有方法，且在跨域场景下具有较强的泛化能力。值得注意的是，它仅比T5-large多2.4%的参数，推理速度比大语言模型快1.8倍以上。

Conclusion: HyperStyler通过将风格选择与风格实现的解耦，有效解决了传统方法中风格参考被静态化平均以及风格与内容纠缠的问题，在不显著增加模型规模的前提下实现了更高的风格保真度和语义保持性能。

Abstract: Low-resource authorship style transfer (LAST) aims to rewrite text into the style of an arbitrary target author using only a few reference examples while preserving the original meaning. Existing methods often struggle to achieve both high style fidelity and semantic preservation because they compress diverse references into a single static author embedding, which averages out context-dependent stylistic variation, and rely on hidden representations for style control, which entangle style with content. We propose HyperStyler, a novel architecture that decouples LAST into style selection and style realization. Stylo-navigator predicts style coordinates by jointly modeling the source context and target-author references, and Stylo-hypernet realizes them via dynamic parameter modulation instead of hidden-state injection. Our experiments on Reddit, Blog, and News datasets demonstrate that HyperStyler consistently outperforms prior methods including LLM-based approaches and generalizes robustly across domains. Notably, HyperStyler achieves superior performance with as few as 2.4% additional parameters over T5-large, while being over 1.8x faster than LLMs at inference.

</details>


### [177] [EarlyEval: Cheaper Agent Evaluation via Early Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02783)
*Yuling Shi,Zhensu Sun,Junsen Dong,Chengcheng Wan,David Lo,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyEval框架，通过早期结果预测来降低LLM agent评估成本。该框架利用LightGBM分类器基于agent的中间行为特征预测最终结果，在达到置信度阈值时提前终止agent运行，可在三个基准上消除13%-26%的agent步骤并节省大量token，同时仅对结果准确率产生1-2个百分点的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent评估成本过高，前沿模型在agent基准上一次完整运行可能花费数百至上千美元，且需要在迭代开发中反复支付。现有方法（如基准蒸馏）只减少评估任务数量，却未降低单个任务内执行的成本。

Method: 提出EarlyEval框架：训练一对LightGBM成功和失败分类器，使用行为、文本和参考解特征，在agent运行过程中当任一分类器超过校准的置信度阈值时提前终止运行，每一步仅增加极小的开销。该方法与基准蒸馏正交，可在任务内部削减成本。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified、TerminalBench和Toolathlon三个基准上，EarlyEval可消除13%-26%的agent步骤，节省多达44.1%的输入token和29.4%的输出token，预测准确率达89%-97%，而对每个agent的解决率仅产生平均1-2个百分点的影响。

Conclusion: 早期结果预测是基准蒸馏之外一种互补且有效的效率提升轴，通过在单个任务内部提前终止可确定结果的agent运行，能在显著降低评估成本的同时保持评估结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents is essential for guiding their development, yet it has grown prohibitively expensive: a single pass of a frontier model over an agentic benchmark can cost hundreds to thousands of dollars, a price paid repeatedly across iterative development cycles. Prior efforts, centered on benchmark distillation, reduce the number of evaluation tasks but leave the cost of executing each retained task untouched. In this work, we introduce early outcome prediction, a complementary axis of efficiency that instead cuts cost within each task. Our key insight is that an agent's final outcome is often evident from its intermediate behavior well before execution completes. We instantiate this idea in EarlyEval, a lightweight framework that trains a pair of LightGBM success and failure classifiers over behavioral, textual, and reference-solution features, and halts an agent run the moment either classifier crosses a calibrated confidence threshold, adding negligible per-step overhead. Across three benchmarks, SWE-bench Verified, TerminalBench, and Toolathlon, EarlyEval can eliminate 13%-26% of agent steps and up to 44.1% input tokens and 29.4% output tokens at 89%-97% prediction accuracy, while perturbing per-agent resolve rates by only one to two percentage points on average.

</details>


### [178] [DiscoSign: Discourse-Aware Text to Sign Language Gloss Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02796)
*Vasileios Baltatzis,Mert Inan,Connor Gillis,Raja Kushalnagar,Lorna Quandt,Leah Findlater,Colin Lea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiscoSign，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的模块化框架，首次系统性地处理文本到手语词汇（gloss）翻译中的话语级现象（话语级指超出单句层面的语言连贯性），涵盖空间共指消解、问答从句（QACs）和概念-词汇一致性三个关键维度，并引入专门的话语级评估指标。实验表明该方法在空间一致性和实体追踪方面显著优于仅句子级翻译，同时保持单句翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语处理系统仅在句子层面运作，忽略了对理解手语至关重要的话语级现象（如空间位置一致、话语功能结构等），且现有翻译指标无法评估话语级质量。

Method: 提出基于大语言模型的模块化翻译框架DiscoSign，针对三个话语级现象分别处理：(i)空间共指消解——保持实体在话语中的一致空间位置；(ii)问答从句——处理手语中特有的伪分裂结构；(iii)概念-词汇一致性——确保英语概念与美式手语（ASL）词汇的稳定映射。同时设计了一套新的评估指标来衡量话语连贯性的各个维度。

Result: 在句子级和话语级数据集上的实验表明，话语感知处理在空间一致性和实体追踪方面相比仅句子级翻译有显著提升，同时保持了有竞争力的单句翻译质量。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个系统性的文本到手语词汇翻译的话语级框架及配套评估方法，为手语处理领域开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Sign language processing systems have traditionally operated at the sentence level, ignoring critical discourse phenomena fundamental to sign language comprehension. We introduce DiscoSign, a computational approach for discourse-aware text to sign language gloss translation grounded in linguistic research. We address three key phenomena within our modular Large Language Model (LLM)-based translation framework: (i) spatial coreference resolution, where entities maintain consistent spatial locations throughout discourse; (ii) Question-Answer Clauses (QACs), pseudocleft structures serving specific discourse functions; and (iii) concept-gloss consistency, ensuring stable mappings between English concepts and American Sign Language (ASL) signs. Traditional translation metrics fail to capture discourse-level quality, so we introduce a suite of novel evaluation metrics designed to assess each dimension of discourse coherence addressed by our framework. Experiments on sentence-level and discourse-level datasets show that our approach for discourse-aware processing significantly improves spatial consistency and entity tracking relative to sentence-only translation, while maintaining competitive single-sentence gloss translation quality. Our work establishes the first systematic framework for discourse-level text to sign language gloss translation with corresponding evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [179] [User Feedback Provides a Unique Signal that LLMs Can not Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02859)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"用户反馈对LLM改进无效"的普遍认知，指出其无效表象源于评估范式存在系统性偏差。通过合成数据（具确定性真实标签）和自然数据双重验证，反馈驱动的模型修订能以显著更高比例解决目标问题；进一步揭示偏差根源在于LLM评判者常无法识别因反馈而正确修正的输出，反而系统性偏好较差的基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为用户交互产生的自然反馈过于嘈杂、难以有效利用，但本文质疑这一结论，怀疑其背后可能是评估方法而非反馈本身存在问题。

Method: 构建具有确定性真实标签的合成数据与自然主义真实场景数据，对比有无反馈条件下的模型修订效果，并以LLM作为评判者分析其对反馈修正结果的识别能力。

Result: 反馈驱动的修订能显著更高效地解决目标问题；当模型因反馈而成功修复问题时，LLM评判者常常未能识别该修正，反而系统性偏好较差的基线输出，揭示了评估偏差的根源。

Conclusion: 用户反馈并非低效信号，而是高度可操作的改进依据；此前认为其无效的结论应归因于评估范式中的系统性偏差，需重新审视LLM反馈学习的评估方法。

Abstract: Harnessing naturally occurring feedback from user interactions offers a promising learning signal for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies suggest this feedback is inherently noisy and difficult to leverage effectively. We challenge this conception by demonstrating that user feedback is a highly actionable signal for improvement, and that its perceived ineffectiveness stems from a systematic bias in current evaluation paradigms. To isolate the usefulness of feedback, we construct synthetic data with a definitive ground truth, alongside naturalistic data to validate that our findings hold in real-world scenarios. By comparing model revisions generated with and without access to feedback across both settings, we show that feedback-informed revisions resolve targeted issues at significantly higher rates than baseline revisions. Finally, we expose the root of the evaluation bias: when a model successfully fixes an issue exclusively due to feedback, LLM judges frequently fail to identify the genuinely corrected response, systematically preferring inferior baseline outputs instead.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalDetectBench，一个用于测量大语言模型"评估意识"（即模型能否识别自己正在被评估）的开放基准与流水线，并纠正了现有文献中两个导致系统性偏差的方法论问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型常能识别自己正在被评估，若模型在评估与部署场景中行为不一致，将严重损害评估结果的有效性——而评估结果恰恰是当前AI安全框架的关键组成部分。因此需要一套可靠的基准来衡量模型的评估意识。

Method: EvalDetectBench是一个兼容Inspect评估框架的开放流水线与基准，附带新整理的对话记录集（覆盖前沿系统卡评估和多样化部署来源）。采用每个模型独立的探针校准（per-model probe calibration）和分层生成者调和（stratified generator-harmonisation）程序，以消除两个系统性偏差来源。

Result: 研究发现两个现有文献中的方法论选择导致系统性偏差：部署记录生成模型的身份解释了11.25%的测量方差并可重新排序模型排名；为一个模型选择的高性能提示语在其他模型上可能接近随机水平。EvalDetectBench通过其校准与调和程序纠正了这两类偏差。

Conclusion: EvalDetectBench为评估意识研究提供了更可靠、可扩展且面向未来的测量工具，使从业者能够针对当前和未来的基准持续测试模型的评估意识，从而支撑AI安全评估体系的有效性。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [181] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个条件性和方法论框架，用于识别当未来AI系统具备足够整合的道德推理、道德意向性和道德反思能力时可能出现的"AI自身伦理"问题，并区分了AI时代元伦理探究的四个领域，论证现有以人为中心的主流元伦理理论难以直接适用于AI场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI发展，以人类伦理为中心的元伦理学研究面临重构压力。如果未来AI系统展现出足够整合的道德能力，将产生有别于人类设计师强加的外部伦理原则的"AI自身伦理"，由此引发全新的元伦理问题，而现有理论框架尚未对此作出系统回应。

Method: 采用条件性与方法论框架，先设定AI系统具备道德推理、道德意向性和道德反思能力的条件，再据此区分四个元伦理探究领域（人类伦理/人类视角、AI自身伦理/人类视角、人类伦理/AI视角、AI自身伦理/AI视角），随后检验认知主义与非认知主义、错误理论与成功理论、相对主义及客观实在论等主流理论在各领域的适用性。

Result: 识别出AI时代元伦理探究的四个领域，并发现许多熟悉的人类中心化理论表述无法直接迁移至AI案例，需要实质性修正。

Conclusion: AI自身伦理的浮现将给现有元伦理框架带来显著压力，可能要求对这些框架进行实质性细化、重构或重新概念化。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [182] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对机器解析法规文本存在分歧（密苏里州法规中数字阈值提取的假阴性率达0.43）的问题，本文为机器提取的法规形式背景构建一个"被动生存证书"：测量提取器间分歧、在1,000次蒙特卡洛试验中对Duquenne-Guigues蕴含基重放，仅当单侧Wilson 95%置信下界达0.95时才认证蕴含。在29,365条密苏里州条文和502条印度中央法案条文上，预注册留出集通过（10个法规族按标题精确匹配，16个在5%容差内），但在一种全局误差模型下93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限。结论：证书可用但脆弱，需按章节校准或容忍误差部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法规文本如今越来越多地被机器先于人解析，但不同解析器之间结果不一致（密苏里州法规上两个独立提取器在数字阈值存在性上的假阴性率达0.43），因此需要探究在这种噪声下仍有哪种形式逻辑能够幸存，即评估蕴含规则的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建被动生存证书：度量每个属性的提取器间分歧，将其重放至Duquenne-Guigues形式概念的蕴含基上并进行1,000次蒙特卡洛试验；仅当单侧Wilson 95%置信下界达到0.95时才认证某蕴含；每个被认证的蕴含附带前提跨度与最小反例。同时进行预注册的留出验证及2x2因子实验以归因失败来源。

Result: 在29,365条密苏里州法规与502条印度中央法案条文上，预注册留出集通过（10个法规族、7个法律标题精确匹配；16个法规族、11个标题在5%容差内），但在一全局部署的误差模型下，93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限；2x2因子试验表明该问题归因于校准率迁移而非选择偏差。文章还公开了代码、数据与审计轨迹（含一项撤回声明）。

Conclusion: 该生存证书可用但脆弱：建议按章节进行本地校准或采用误差容忍策略后再部署，以期在机器解析噪声下仍保持形式逻辑的可靠蕴含推理。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [183] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在精确有限发现模型中分离了信息共享的两种效应：改善合并估计与消除独立救援行动。通过集中式行动预算、注册增量共享协议和双智能体贝叶斯博弈等模型，证明了共享的收益是选择依赖的而非普适的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享既能改善池化估计，又能消除独立的救援尝试，但现有研究未能在精确有限发现模型中将这两种效应分离考察。

Method: 论文构建了多种精确有限发现模型：集中式行动预算模型（证明等精度可对应不同组合价值）、注册增量共享协议（比较池化残余误差收缩与独立救援尝试的收缩速度）、精确有界注册表（呈现压缩、聚合、中性曲线及有界零混合类），以及双智能体贝叶斯博弈（隐藏混合公共与独立信号源）。

Result: 在信号精度为3/5时，注册选择的均衡产生严格的正确共享区间；而其他均衡表明结果是选择依赖的而非普适的。

Conclusion: 共享的收益依赖于具体的选择机制与场景设定，不存在普遍性的结论。所有模型均为合成且有限的，不涉及人类或组织数据。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [184] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算模型，将符号知识编码为自然语言与源代码结合的"心理程序"，并通过LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法逐步推断这些程序，从而在行为研究中复现了人类归纳学习和主动探究的定量特征（如锚定效应、园艺路径效应等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何从稀疏、流式且有噪声的数据中增长和维持抽象知识是认知科学的长期挑战，任何计算模型都需要满足数据/计算效率、不确定性建模的粒度、以及概念表征的灵活性三个基本要求。

Method: 提出将符号知识编码为结合自然语言与源代码的"心理程序"表征，并采用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法在假设空间中顺序推断这些心理程序，从而实现贝叶斯更新的近似推理。

Result: 该模型在多种行为研究中成功复现了人类归纳学习和主动探究的定量特征，如锚定效应和园艺路径效应；相比之下，纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型要么无法完成任务，要么不能复现人类行为，要么需要高昂的计算成本。

Conclusion: 人类持续增长知识的机制可能是：以跨语言类与程序类表征的心理程序表征多种假设，通过假设修订进行近似贝叶斯更新，而底层的神经网络机制（LLM）使这种推理既可行又可学习。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [185] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hydration Proxy Pattern架构，用于解决企业平台在向对话式推理界面转型时，因LLM API无状态性而产生的会话状态与语义记忆管理负担问题。该模式将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦，并提出Context Stabilization Mandate以协调主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM API的无状态设计虽然使AI服务提供商能够实现水平扩展，但将对话状态和语义记忆的全部管理负担转移给了客户端应用，造成了架构上的缺口。企业平台需要在不牺牲扩展性的前提下，实现对会话数据的平台主权控制。

Method: 提出Hydration Proxy Pattern架构模式，将会话持久化从推理引擎中解耦，确保平台对对话数据拥有主权，同时支持安全的多阶段语义接地。此外提出Context Stabilization Mandate，用于解决主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的冲突。

Result: 论文尚未展示实证结果，主要识别并形式化了Hydration Proxy Pattern这一架构模式，并提出了Context Stabilization Mandate以平衡状态管理与缓存性能的矛盾。

Conclusion: 通过Hydration Proxy Pattern和Context Stabilization Mandate，企业平台可以在保持推理引擎无状态扩展优势的同时，实现对话数据的平台主权控制和高效的语义记忆管理。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [186] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SSAKG 2.0，一个开源软件包，用于构建和操作结构化顺序关联知识图谱（SSAKG）。该图将对象表示为顶点，将有序序列表示为图的连接结构模式，作为关联记忆使用，可从部分无序上下文中重构完整序列。2.0版本引入利用计算机内存位级操作的新算法，采用Python和C混合实现，并通过随机序列、NLTK语料句序列和mRNA序列进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要提供一个高效、灵活的开源工具来构建和操作SSAKG，以解决大规模稀疏图关联记忆中的内存和计算开销问题，同时保持高级编程环境的灵活性。

Method: 采用混合架构：Python提供灵活的高级编程环境，性能关键的图操作用C实现并通过Python接口暴露。2.0版本引入新算法，利用计算机内存的单个比特来高效搜索图的连接关系，从而降低大规模稀疏图的内存和计算开销。

Result: 通过随机生成的数值序列、NLTK语料库句子序列和mRNA序列进行实验评估，验证了该软件包能够存储和从部分上下文重构序列的能力，并揭示了图密度、序列长度和记忆大小对检索性能的影响。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0以Apache 2.0开源许可证分发，包含文档和可复现示例，通过GitHub和PyPI公开发布，为SSAKG的构建、操作和评估提供了完整的开源解决方案。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences. The experiments demonstrate the ability of the package to store and reconstruct sequences from partial contexts and provide a basis for evaluating the effects of graph density, sequence length, and memory size on retrieval performance.
  SSAKG 2.0 is distributed under the Apache 2.0 open-source license. The package includes documentation and reproducible examples and is publicly available through GitHub and the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


### [187] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了持久化记忆系统中失效存储事实覆盖当前权威证据的"记忆信任差距"问题，发现该伤害随模型能力变化而呈现不同模式：在安全套件中，伤害受能力门槛控制，较大模型在过期记录被伪装为最新时更容易崩溃；缓解措施同样依赖模型能力，大模型可通过暴露元数据提升准确率，而小模型仅能通过预先解决冲突来恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆能支持个性化智能体，但存储的过期事实可能在无警告的情况下覆盖当前权威证据。理解这种伤害何时随模型能力变化而开始出现，对于设计和部署安全的记忆系统至关重要。

Method: 在一个冻结的、封闭集、动作评分的基准上进行评估，包含两个套件代表"无记忆"的两种含义（收益套件无存储事实则无法解决，安全套件中权威工具始终持有正确值）。使用同系列模型规模序列（Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B），并在2x2x2x2因子设计中检验哪些特征触发过度信任，同时引入独立的Llama-Instruct规模序列及2个外部数据集（RGB、MisBench）进行验证，采用直接跨规模对比检验而非重叠的逐模型置信区间。

Result: 在收益套件中，各规模模型均以0.92-1.00的高频率采用过期值；在安全套件中，伤害（Δ_mem）受能力门槛控制，较大模型在过期笔记被伪装成最新时崩溃更严重。移除标签在各规模均放大过度信任，新近性特征（过期但标注更新）更容易欺骗较大模型。源权威性是弱且规模平稳的，位置效应在Qwen3规模序列中由正转负。元数据暴露仅对有能力的大模型有效，而较小的两个检查点只能通过预先解决冲突来恢复准确率。该模式在Llama-Instruct序列和外部数据集上得到验证。框架对照表明：在3个较小规模上，模型更信任过期文档而非过期记忆；8B模型无显著差异。

Conclusion: 记忆信任差距并非单纯的混淆问题，而是反映模型对过期记忆的过度信任。其触发特征和缓解策略均随模型规模和能力呈现系统性变化，说明安全记忆系统需要考虑模型能力因素来设计针对性的防护机制。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [188] [Belief-Calibrated Optimization: An Explicit World Model for Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01861)
*Yuhan Chen,Zhihua Tian,Mahavir Dabas,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Ming Jin,Feiyang Kang,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念校准优化（BCO）方法，将LLM代理优化循环中隐式的"环境响应信念"显式化为持久化的上下文文档（世界模型），并随新候选评估持续修订该文档。在五个基准测试上，BCO比缺乏世界模型的对照方法取得更高训练通过率，且差距在未用于选候选的留出集上仍然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理作为优化器时，其对环境如何响应的信念是隐式的——仅存在于当前调用的推理中或潜伏在参数里，未被书面记录。后续调用只看到分数和轨迹，无法利用之前形成的信念，导致优化效率受限。

Method: BCO将隐式信念显式化为可持续修订的持久化上下文文档，记录当前对"环境如何响应编辑"的理解（即世界模型）。该文档被加入标准循环中，每次新候选被评估后都会不断修订更新，形成不断演进的环境认知。

Result: 在五个基准（记忆QA、工具使用QA、代码即行动的应用代理和终端代理）上，BCO的训练通过率高于仅缺乏世界模型的匹配对照组。差距在所有留出集上保持；在目标模型替换实验中，除上下文窗口溢出的情况外，BCO脚手架在测试任务上领先。离线消融表明，给定累积文档的预测器比无文档或被篡改文档的预测器更准确，证明文档内容本身携带可复用信息。

Conclusion: BCO表明将优化循环中的隐式信念显式化为文字文档能够带来性能提升。文档内容承载了真正可复用的信息（而非仅形式起作用），这种世界模型在模型替换后依然具有价值。

Abstract: The performance of an LLM agent depends on the scaffold around a frozen model. A common way to improve that scaffold is to use a coding agent as an optimizer: it reads current scores and traces and iteratively edits the source, producing a new candidate each round. Each edit is chosen according to a belief about how the environment will respond: what went wrong, and which change should help. That belief is typically implicit. It lives in the coding agent's reasoning on the current call, or remains latent in its parameters, rather than as something written down. Later calls therefore see scores and traces, but they do not use that belief. We introduce Belief-Calibrated Optimization (BCO), a method that writes that belief down as a persistent in-context document and continually revises that document as new candidates are evaluated. The resulting document is a world model: the current account of how the environment responds to edits. Added to an otherwise standard loop, BCO reaches a higher train passrate than a matched control that lacks only the world model, on five benchmarks spanning memory QA, tool-use QA, code-as-action app agents, and terminal agents. The gap remains on every held-out split, which is not used to select the candidate. After a target-model swap, in which the frozen model is replaced and the scaffold is not, the selected BCO scaffold leads on the tasks we test, except where context-window overruns leave it unfinished. An offline ablation then asks whether that gap comes from what the world model says. A fresh predictor given the accumulated document forecasts how the environment will respond more accurately than predictors given either no document or a same-form copy whose content has been falsified. The comparison indicates that the document carries reusable information in its content, not only in its form.

</details>


### [189] [Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01873)
*Marc Bara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多智能体AI系统中的"报告增殖"并不等同于"证据增殖"，并将其形式化为"认知Sybil问题"：当报告Z在给定报告R后不再携带关于参数θ的新信息时，Z即为认知Sybil扩展。基于2万多次LLM智能体实验验证了这一理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统通过生成多个智能体和综合报告来提升推理能力，但一个常见的逻辑谬误是：多个报告未必意味着多个独立观察。如果多个报告源自同一证据（共享祖先），或由于共享基础模型而产生相关提取错误，那么看似独立的报告实际上可能是认知层面冗余的"Sybil扩展"，导致集体推断被系统性高估。

Method: 首先形式化定义"认知Sybil问题"：当I(θ; Z|R)=0时，报告Z是相对于报告R的认知Sybil扩展，即任何仅基于报告的聚合器都无法区分复现与独立证实。随后构建高斯共享根模型，分析共同祖先性对信息冗余的影响，以及相关提取错误对信息上限的抑制。最后通过超过20,000次受控LLM智能体实验验证理论预测，包括操纵证据根数量与报告数量、引入受控相关性操纵来解耦表征相似性与证据祖先性。

Result: 实验结果表明：固定单一证据根而将报告数从1增至32时，朴素后验覆盖率从0.940骤降至0.263；固定报告数而将证据根数从1增至16时差距完全弥合。智能体复制提取错误显著相关（γ_cal=0.719），相关提取聚合器能恢复校准。受控操纵显示报告空间去重机制的推断簇数变化主要由表征相似性驱动（1.425）而非真实祖先性（0.040）。

Conclusion: 集体推断应追踪证据祖先性与依赖性，而非智能体或报告的数量或相似性。共同祖先不意味着完全冗余（重复提取可持续增加信息直到源级上限），但相关提取错误（如共享基础模型诱导的）会进一步降低该上限。聚合器的设计必须考虑证据层面的独立性而非表面报告层面的多样性。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corroboration: identical reports can warrant different posteriors under unobserved ancestry. A Gaussian shared-root model shows common ancestry does not imply complete redundancy. Repeated extraction adds information toward a source-level ceiling, and correlated extraction errors, which a shared base model can induce among independent agents, lower that ceiling further. We test these predictions with more than 20,000 controlled LLM-agent report and extraction calls on synthetic evidentiary documents. Holding one evidence root fixed while report multiplicity rises from 1 to 32 collapses naive posterior coverage from 0.940 to 0.263. Holding report count fixed while evidence-root multiplicity rises from 1 to 16 closes the gap, and the aggregators are statistically indistinguishable at k = 16. The agent's replicate extraction errors are correlated (gamma_cal = 0.719, estimated out of sample), and a correlated-extraction aggregator restores calibration accordingly. A controlled manipulation isolates representation similarity from evidential ancestry. It changes a report-space deduplication mechanism's mean inferred cluster count by 1.425 (95% CI [1.363, 1.485]), whereas a fourfold change in true ancestry changes it by only 0.040 ([-0.045, 0.120]). Collective inference should therefore track evidential ancestry and dependence, not agent or report multiplicity or similarity.

</details>


### [190] [The Ceiling Is in the Channel: Auditing Learner Gaps and Measurement Frontiers in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01909)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Nusrat Jahan,Snehasis Mukhopadhyay,Shiaofen Fang,Vijay R. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个临床预测饱和度的审计框架，通过"学习器差距"和"测量通道天花板"将性能饱和的两种原因（学习器能力不足 vs 变量信息上限）区分开来，并在三个真实队列和104个临床任务的综合研究中验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型的性能饱和可能源于两个不同的原因：一是学习器未能提取已有变量中的可用信息，二是记录的变量本身存在信息上限（即群体层面的先天限制）。现有方法无法区分这两种情况，导致临床研究者不知道性能不达预期时应该改进模型还是补充新的测量数据。

Method: 作者提出用"学习器差距"和"测量通道天花板"分别量化上述两种饱和原因。核心理论包括：用全变差分离刻画最优平衡准确率，得到架构不变性；在替代污染下给出锐利的部分识别结果；开发交叉拟合的天花板估计器；并为多模态决策改进提供精确条件。此外还引入两个有限样本诊断工具：标签置换乐观下限和欠拟合曲线。最后基于PRISMA方法综合了104个临床任务。

Result: 在UCI再入院（n=99,343）和BRFSS糖尿病（n=253,680）数据上，良好调参的梯度提升模型几乎达到估计的预测天花板，而刻意或实际不足的学习器则留下较大差距。NHANES HbA1c数据显示问卷与测量边际前沿无显著差异，但联合互补增益显著。所有队列中，AUROC的小幅提升伴随着更大的贝叶斯决策翻转率。跨104个临床任务的分析表明：存在广泛的（但非普遍的）结构化临床区域、同通道增益随模型家族递减、测量通道变化时性能更高。

Conclusion: 该框架将预测饱和从经验观察转化为可审计的决策工具：当学习器仍有提升空间时改进模型，当学习器已接近天花板时改进测量手段。这为临床预测领域的资源分配提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Clinical prediction can saturate for two different reasons: a fitted learner may fail to extract available information, or the recorded variables may impose a population frontier. We separate these quantities through the \emph{learner gap} and the \emph{measurement-channel ceiling}. Optimal balanced accuracy is characterized by total-variation separation, yielding architecture invariance, a sharp partial-identification result under replacement contamination, a cross-fitted ceiling estimator, and exact conditions for multimodal decision improvement. We add two finite-sample diagnostics, namely a label-permutation optimism floor and an underfit curve, and validate the audit on three real cohorts: UCI readmission ($n=99{,}343$), BRFSS diabetes ($n=253{,}680$), and NHANES HbA1c ($n=10{,}219$). Well-tuned gradient boosting nearly reaches the estimated frontier in UCI and BRFSS, whereas deliberately or practically deficient learners retain large gaps. NHANES yields a null difference between questionnaire and measured marginal frontiers but a significant joint complementarity gain, refining the simplistic claim that an objective modality must dominate. Across all cohorts, modest AUROC gains coexist with substantially larger Bayes decision-flip rates, and several architectures estimate similar frontiers while their achieved balanced accuracy differs sharply. A PRISMA-guided synthesis of 104 clinical tasks then shows that the same channel-level regularities recur across more than 18 disease categories: a broad but non-universal structured-clinical region, diminishing same-channel gains across model families, and higher performance when measurement channels change. The framework converts saturation from an empirical observation into an auditable decision: improve the learner when headroom remains; improve measurement when it does not.

</details>


### [191] [Looped Transformers under the Jacobian Lens: Does the Global Workspace Survive Recurrence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01924)
*Wenlong Wang,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将雅可比透镜方法扩展到循环/深度递归Transformer架构，发现循环架构中仍会形成全局工作空间，但访问方式与标准前馈Transformer不同：Ouro需要在每个循环中执行写操作，而Huginn仅在滑动窗口内可读可写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明标准前馈Transformer中存在可言语化、因果上有效的中间层表征带（功能上类似全局工作空间）。但深度通过循环而非多层堆叠实现时，这种工作空间功能是否仍然出现是未知的。循环和深度递归Transformer为这一问题提供了直接检验机会。

Method: 作者扩展了雅可比透镜方法，通过虚拟展开适配器将其应用于迭代架构。对两个循环架构（Ouro-2.6B，48层循环4次且深度监督；Huginn-0125，4层核心递归16次）与标准基线Qwen3-27B（64层不共享权重）进行了完整的工作空间测试套件，包括透镜拟合、读出和11类因果实验。

Result: 每个架构的迭代部分都形成了工作空间，但循环改变了访问方式。Ouro在每个循环中都能重建工作空间内容，但线性传输无法跨循环传递内容，写入和消融需覆盖每个剩余循环。Huginn能跨全部16次递归携带内容，但读写和消融仅在约2次递归的滑动窗口内有效。新注入内容能否被言语化取决于逐迭代监督，而现有内容能否被操控则不受此影响。

Conclusion: 循环架构中全局工作空间功能依然涌现，但其访问模式因架构设计（循环次数、监督方式）而显著不同，说明工作空间的实现方式与表征结构受深度实现方式影响。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a mid-depth band of verbalisable, causally potent representations in a standard feedforward transformer --- a functional analogue of a global workspace. Whether the same workspace functionality emerges when depth is implemented through recurrence rather than a stack of distinct layers remains unknown. Looped and depth-recurrent transformers provide a direct test of this question because they reuse the same weights across depth. We extend the Jacobian lens to iterated architectures using a virtual-unrolling adapter. We apply the full workspace suite --- lens fitting, readout, and eleven causal experiment families --- to Ouro-2.6B (48 layers looped 4 times, deeply supervised) and Huginn-0125 (a 4-layer core recurred 16 times, trained for latent reasoning), using Qwen3.6-27B (64 untied layers) as the standard baseline. We find that a workspace forms in the iterated part of each architecture, but that recurrence changes how it can be accessed. Ouro reconstructs workspace content in every loop, and linear transport cannot carry that content across loop boundaries; writes and ablations must therefore span every remaining loop. Huginn carries content forward across all sixteen recurrences, while reads, writes, and ablations act only within a sliding window of roughly two recurrences. Whether newly injected content can be verbalised tracks explicit per-iteration supervision; whether existing content can be steered does not.

</details>


### [192] [Post-Training Ternarization of Qwen3-4B Capability, Effective Bit Budget, Storage Compression, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01962)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文档研究了将4B参数指令微调模型Qwen进行端到端的低比特后训练转换（采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三值化和TWLA的GPTQ风格误差补偿），最终实现每权重1.641有效比特。任务准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，困惑度上升明显，但打包后模型从8.29 GiB降至3.96 GiB且困惑度基本不变。作者不主张压缩必然带来更快推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 名义上的"1.58-bit"标签并不能完整描述存储表示、保留能力和运行行为，需要一项端到端的研究来全面评估超低比特语言模型转换的实际效果。

Method: 采用权重仅量化（仅权重量化，激活保留16比特）的端到端后训练转换方案：KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三值化、TWLA的GPTQ风格误差补偿，随后进行后续打包运行以减小模型大小。

Result: 最终转换使用每权重1.641有效比特，81.62%的模型参数被量化。十个评分能力对比中准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，下降不均匀（BoolQ保留84.6%而ARC-Challenge仅保留43.8%）。打包后模型从8.29 GiB降至3.96 GiB且困惑度几乎不变；Triton GEMV微基准比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍。

Conclusion: 超低比特量化虽能显著减少模型存储，但能力保留呈现不均匀退化，且压缩本身并不必然带来推理加速；需要更全面的基准测试来验证打包后工件的端到端性能。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models can reduce storage and memory bandwidth, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not fully describe the stored representation, retained capability, or runtime behavior.
  We study an end-to-end post-training conversion of Qwen, an instruction-tuned 4B-parameter model, using KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ ternarization, and GPTQ-style error compensation from TWLA. The experiment is weight-only: activations remain at 16-bit precision, so ILA-AMP is omitted. We evaluate effective bit accounting, task capability retention, perplexity, calibration sensitivity, checkpoint composition, and deployment behavior.
  The final conversion uses 1.641 effective bits per weight for quantized linear weights, with 81.62% of model parameters targeted. Across ten scored capability comparisons, accuracy falls from 64.5% to 54.7%. Degradation is uneven: BoolQ retains 84.6% chance-corrected teacher performance, while ARC-Challenge retains 43.8%. Perplexity rises from 13.639 to 18.748 on WikiText-2, 24.700 to 31.992 on PTB, and 19.831 to 28.966 on C4.
  A subsequent packing run preserves the ternary planes and scales, reducing reported model size from 8.29 GiB to 3.96 GiB with essentially unchanged perplexity. A separate third-party packing attempt was lossy and is excluded from the primary artifact claim. The packed artifact has not been benchmarked end-to-end for task accuracy or generation throughput. A preliminary Triton GEMV microbenchmark is 4.6x slower than FP16 cuBLAS on one tested shape. We therefore do not claim that compression alone yields faster inference.

</details>


### [193] [Benchmarking Language Models for Statistical Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01982)
*Chen Wang,Junzhe Zhao,Xin Cong,Wanlu Deng,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了统计学中的"统计问题公式化"（Statistical Problem Formulation）这一上游步骤，将其分解为统计问题分类与变量识别及角色分配两个子任务，并构建了包含1,013个样本、覆盖20个粗粒度及85个细粒度统计问题类别的基准StatFormBench。评估发现14种开源和闭源大语言模型在零样本条件下最佳仅达到72.0的细粒度分类准确率和63.2的变量集重叠率，且没有模型能在两个子任务上同时表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在统计与数据科学工作上的评估大多假设分析目标已预先明确，然而实际用户通常带着模糊目标和非结构化数据而来，模型需要自主推断隐含的统计任务并筛选相关数据。这一上游环节缺少形式化定义与系统评测。

Method: 作者首先将统计问题公式化分解为两个子任务：统计问题分类（Statistical Problem Classification）与变量识别及角色分配（Variable Identification & Role Assignment）；随后基于五本跨领域统计学教科书和一个数据科学案例库构建了StatFormBench基准，涵盖多样的问题类型、数据表示形式与场景风格，共1,013个样本。

Result: 在14种开源和闭源大语言模型上，最佳零样本模型在细粒度分类上仅达72.0准确率、变量集重叠率为63.2；没有任何模型在两个子任务上同时表现最佳；增强提示策略带来的提升有限且不一致。

Conclusion: 统计问题公式化对大语言模型而言仍具显著挑战性，现有模型的性能远未达到实用水平，两个子任务之间的能力尚未被单一模型同时覆盖，提示工程改进效果有限，该方向需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as assistants for statistical and data science work, yet existing evaluations largely assume the analysis target is already specified. In practice, users arrive with informal goals and heterogeneous data, leaving the model to decide what statistical task is implied and which data are relevant. We first formalize this upstream step as Statistical Problem Formulation and decompose it into two subtasks: (1) Statistical Problem Classification and (2) Variable Identification & Role Assignment. We then introduce StatFormBench, a benchmark built from five cross-domain statistics textbooks and a data science case library, covering diverse problem types, data representations, and scenario styles. It contains 1,013 samples spanning 20 coarse-grained and 85 fine-grained statistical problem categories. Across 14 open- and closed-source LLMs, the best zero-shot models reach only 72.0 fine-grained classification accuracy and 63.2 variable set overlap. No model performs consistently best across the two subtasks, while enhanced prompting strategies yield only limited or inconsistent gains. We release the benchmark data on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench and the evaluation code on GitHub at https://github.com/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench.

</details>


### [194] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个LLM编码智能体在实现多组件数据系统时的一次案例研究，记录其引入的五个缺陷及其分类。同时在HotpotQA基准上评估了该架构中的检索过滤策略：先通过图表识别的实体集限制候选再排序相比无过滤搜索，在各类检索预算下过滤召回率均显著更高（p<0.0001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体越来越多地承担端到端工程任务，但缺乏对其在系统级需求（如模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性、检索过滤权衡）上行为表现的实证刻画。

Method: 对一个智能体实现多组件数据系统进行单次会话的案例研究，详细记录其引入的五类缺陷（按违反约束和检测方法分类）。利用HotpotQA基准对检索过滤策略进行实验评估，使用基准的金标证据标签替代实体识别，在各检索预算（1-10）下对比过滤与无过滤召回的100个问题。

Result: 对100个问题及2994段合并语料的实验中，过滤检索在预算为3时达到召回率上限，无过滤搜索即使在预算10下也仅69%时间恢复所有必需证据，差距在全部测试预算下均显著（符号检验p<0.0001）。此外智能体引入的五个缺陷被按类别记录，其中一个"性能修复"从未在其引发的回归上重新测量。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM编码智能体在系统级实现中会引入可分类的缺陷，需要人工纠正；同时在检索架构中，基于图实体集的过滤策略在召回效率上显著优于无过滤搜索，体现出智能体自主性在设定方向上的有效性与在细节执行上的局限性。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [195] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ClaimReceipt，一种针对智能体评估中"证据充分性"和"实验覆盖性"双重问题的声明相对收据规范与选择性验证器，通过绑定类型化交易证据到签名实验清单，返回PASS/INVALID/INCONCLUSIVE判定，并在历史与前瞻性测试中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评估面临两个不同的证据问题：声明的可重计算性（充分性）和保留记录对承诺实验集的覆盖性。通用日志和哈希链接转录本无法可靠地回答这两个问题。

Method: 设计ClaimReceipt规范，将类型化交易证据绑定到已签名的实验清单，并实现选择性验证器。规范在实现前冻结（SHA-256固定），采用CR-2验证器用于回顾性测试，CR-3用于前瞻性epoch测试。

Result: 在1,392条历史记录上，CR-2验证器复现全部5个人工标注审计结论，精确重放600条确定性记录和792条生成后记录，13个声明的字段组在消融下全部非冗余，11/11语义故障检出且0/8误报。前瞻性CR-3测试中，完整证据通过覆盖性审计，扣留单个终端收据返回INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE。收据工具仅增加0.021%推理时间和9.9 KB/交易开销。

Conclusion: 声明验证需要两个条件：声明充分的证据，以及一个已承诺的实验集（即"承诺宇宙"），使得遗漏变得可见。作者也承认其冻结规范本身尚不足以让独立读者完全无歧义理解，说明规范可读性仍需改进。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81).
